**[01:18:41.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000869)  
In addition to privatization,

**[01:18:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000870)  
we are also maximizing returns

**[01:18:45.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000871)  
from government-owned

**[01:18:46.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000872)  
or controlled corporations.

**[01:18:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000873)  
This year,

**[01:18:49.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000874)  
as we have last year,

**[01:18:51.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000875)  
we have called on GOCCs

**[01:18:53.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000876)  
to continue remitting dividends

**[01:18:55.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000877)  
at 75% of their annual net earnings

**[01:18:58.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000878)  
to the national government.

**[01:19:02.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000879)  
Disbursements for the first half of 2026

**[01:19:05.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000880)  
amounted to 3.175 trillion

**[01:19:08.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000881)  
or 49% of the full program.

**[01:19:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000882)  
As resources are limited

**[01:19:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000883)  
and the country's needs continue to grow,

**[01:19:16.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000884)  
we must be more deliberate

**[01:19:17.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000885)  
in directing public funds

**[01:19:19.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000886)  
towards higher quality expenditures

**[01:19:22.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000887)  
with greater multiplier effects.

**[01:19:25.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000888)  
Those that strengthen productive capacity,

**[01:19:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000889)  
stimulate economic activity,

**[01:19:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000890)  
and deliver long-term benefits

**[01:19:33.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000891)  
to the people.

**[01:19:35.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000892)  
The objective is not to spend less,

**[01:19:37.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000893)  
it is to spend better.

**[01:19:40.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000894)  
Because of these efforts,

**[01:19:42.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000895)  
our fiscal deficit

**[01:19:44.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000896)  
has continued to narrow.

**[01:19:46.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000897)  
From 8.6% in 2021

**[01:19:50.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000898)  
and 7.3% in 2022,

**[01:19:54.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000899)  
we have consistently brought down

**[01:19:56.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000900)  
the fiscal deficit

**[01:19:58.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000901)  
to 5.6%

**[01:20:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000902)  
over the last four years.

**[01:20:06.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000903)  
And we continue to make progress

**[01:20:08.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000904)  
this 2026

**[01:20:09.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000905)  
despite the economic shocks

**[01:20:10.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000906)  
that we have faced,

**[01:20:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000907)  
including those arising

**[01:20:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000908)  
from the conflict in the Middle East.

**[01:20:15.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000909)  
In the first half,

**[01:20:17.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000910)  
our deficit-to-GDP ratio

**[01:20:19.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000911)  
improved to 5.46%

**[01:20:22.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000912)  
compared with 5.65%

**[01:20:25.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000913)  
in the same period last year.

**[01:20:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000914)  
And we remain on track

**[01:20:29.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000915)  
to meet our fiscal deficit target

**[01:20:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000916)  
for 2026.

**[01:20:34.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000917)  
We project a deficit-to-GDP ratio

**[01:20:37.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000918)  
of 5.44%

**[01:20:38.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000919)  
in the second half of 2026,

**[01:20:41.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000920)  
bringing the full year

**[01:20:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000921)  
to 5.45%

**[01:20:45.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000922)  
our lowest deficit-to-GDP ratio

**[01:20:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000923)  
since the start of the administration.

**[01:20:50.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000924)  
This continued reduction

**[01:20:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000925)  
in the deficit

**[01:20:53.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000926)  
is an important indicator

**[01:20:55.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000927)  
that the fiscal discipline

**[01:20:57.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000928)  
we are pursuing

**[01:20:58.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000929)  
is translating into concrete results.

**[01:21:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000930)  
And because we continue to manage

**[01:21:03.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000931)  
our fiscal deficit responsibly,

**[01:21:05.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000932)  
our debt levels remain sustainable.

**[01:21:08.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000933)  
In 2025,

**[01:21:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000934)  
the national government debt

**[01:21:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000935)  
stood at 63.2% of GDP,

**[01:21:17.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000936)  
while the general government debt

**[01:21:20.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000937)  
finished at 56.8% of GDP,

**[01:21:25.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000938)  
well below the 70% international benchmark

**[01:21:29.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000939)  
by the World Bank

**[01:21:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000940)  
for debt sustainability

**[01:21:33.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000941)  
and within a manageable range

**[01:21:35.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000942)  
compared to other emerging economies.

**[01:21:39.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000943)  
Just a point I'd like to raise

**[01:21:42.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000944)  
or stress is that

**[01:21:45.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000945)  
the definition of the 70% threshold

**[01:21:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000946)  
is based on general government debt

**[01:21:51.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000947)  
to GDP,

**[01:21:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000948)  
which we are at 56.8%.

**[01:21:57.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000949)  
More importantly,

**[01:21:58.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000950)  
on our debt,

**[01:21:59.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000951)  
we have maintained

**[01:22:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000952)  
a prudent debt mix,

**[01:22:03.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000953)  
predominantly domestic debt,

**[01:22:06.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000954)  
predominantly carrying

**[01:22:08.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000955)  
fixed interest rates,

**[01:22:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000956)  
and predominantly structured

**[01:22:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000957)  
with long repayment terms.

**[01:22:16.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000958)  
First,

**[01:22:17.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000959)  
67% of our outstanding debt

**[01:22:20.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000960)  
is domestic.

**[01:22:21.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000961)  
This reduces our exposure

**[01:22:23.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000962)  
to foreign exchange risks,

**[01:22:25.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000963)  
while also supporting

**[01:22:26.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000964)  
the continued development

**[01:22:27.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000965)  
of our local capital market.

**[01:22:30.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000966)  
Second,

**[01:22:32.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000967)  
90% of our debt

**[01:22:33.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000968)  
carries fixed interest rates.

**[01:22:36.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000969)  
This provides greater certainty

**[01:22:38.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000970)  
in our debt servicing costs

**[01:22:40.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000971)  
and shields us from sudden increases

**[01:22:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000972)  
or fluctuations in global interest rates.

**[01:22:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000973)  
And third,

**[01:22:49.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000974)  
84.1% of our debt

**[01:22:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000975)  
has long-term repayment periods,

**[01:22:55.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000976)  
meaning we have several years

**[01:22:57.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000977)  
before these obligations fall due.

**[01:23:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000978)  
This longer maturity profile

**[01:23:02.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000979)  
reduces our refinancing risks

**[01:23:04.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000980)  
and gives us greater predictability

**[01:23:06.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000981)  
in managing our debt obligations.

**[01:23:10.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000982)  
This strategic borrowing mix

**[01:23:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000983)  
by our Bureau of Treasury

**[01:23:15.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000984)  
strengthens our resilience

**[01:23:17.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000985)  
to external shocks

**[01:23:18.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000986)  
and makes our debt obligations

**[01:23:20.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000987)  
more predictable and manageable

**[01:23:22.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000988)  
over the long term.

**[01:23:24.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000989)  
And the market,

**[01:23:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000990)  
the global markets assessment

**[01:23:30.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000991)  
of our fiscal fundamentals

**[01:23:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000992)  
is also reflected

**[01:23:33.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000993)  
in the confidence

**[01:23:34.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000994)  
of international investors.

**[01:23:38.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000995)  
Our sovereign credit

**[01:23:40.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000996)  
ratings remain

**[01:23:41.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000997)  
at investment-grade levels.

**[01:23:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000998)  
We have two A-minus ratings

**[01:23:45.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-000999)  
from Japanese credit rating agencies

**[01:23:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001000)  
and the equivalent of BBB

**[01:23:51.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001001)  
or BBB Plus

**[01:23:53.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001002)  
from the global rating agencies.

**[01:23:56.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001003)  
This reflects continued confidence

**[01:23:59.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001004)  
in the country's economic fundamentals

**[01:24:01.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001005)  
and our capacity

**[01:24:03.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001006)  
to manage our public's finances.

**[01:24:06.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001007)  
Higher credit ratings reflect

**[01:24:08.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001008)  
minimal default risk

**[01:24:10.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001009)  
signaling lenders to offer

**[01:24:12.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001010)  
lower interest rates.

**[01:24:14.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001011)  
When the country can borrow

**[01:24:15.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001012)  
at lower interest rates,

**[01:24:17.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001013)  
private businesses and consumers

**[01:24:20.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001014)  
can also borrow

**[01:24:21.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001015)  
at lower interest rates.

**[01:24:26.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001016)  
We have also seen progress

**[01:24:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001017)  
in our integration

**[01:24:29.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001018)  
into global capital markets.

**[01:24:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001019)  
With the upcoming historic debut

**[01:24:33.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001020)  
of our peso-denominated

**[01:24:35.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001021)  
government bonds

**[01:24:36.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001022)  
in the J.P. Morgan's

**[01:24:38.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001023)  
Government Bond Index,

**[01:24:39.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001024)  
we will be the first

**[01:24:41.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001025)  
government bond index

**[01:24:42.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001026)  
emerging markets

**[01:24:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001027)  
in January 2027.

**[01:24:45.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001028)  
This is significant

**[01:24:46.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001029)  
because this is our first-ever inclusion

**[01:24:49.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001030)  
in a major global bond index.

**[01:24:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001031)  
And again,

**[01:24:53.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001032)  
this reflects a strong

**[01:24:54.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001033)  
vote of confidence

**[01:24:55.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001034)  
in our solid fundamentals

**[01:24:57.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001035)  
and fiscal discipline.

**[01:25:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001036)  
As we widen our investor base,

**[01:25:02.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001037)  
this will strengthen demand

**[01:25:03.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001038)  
for Philippine government securities,

**[01:25:06.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001039)  
improve market liquidity,

**[01:25:08.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001040)  
support the development

**[01:25:10.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001041)  
of our capital market,

**[01:25:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001042)  
and help lower borrowing costs

**[01:25:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001043)  
for the government over time.

**[01:25:18.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001044)  
In 2027,

**[01:25:21.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001045)  
when we project borrowing requirements

**[01:25:23.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001046)  
to reach 3.3 trillion pesos,

**[01:25:26.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001047)  
we will carry the same principles

**[01:25:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001048)  
of our debt strategy

**[01:25:30.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001049)  
into our borrowing program

**[01:25:32.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001050)  
and make it even more sustainable.

**[01:25:35.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001051)  
We are increasing the share

**[01:25:37.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001052)  
of domestic borrowing

**[01:25:39.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001053)  
to 72%,

**[01:25:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001054)  
which means around 2.4 trillion pesos

**[01:25:45.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001055)  
will be sourced

**[01:25:46.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001056)  
from the domestic market,

**[01:25:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001057)  
while the remaining 28%,

**[01:25:50.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001058)  
or around 915 billion,

**[01:25:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001059)  
will come from external sources,

**[01:25:55.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001060)  
such as loans and global bonds.

**[01:25:59.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001061)  
This borrowing strategy

**[01:26:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001062)  
allows us to keep

**[01:26:01.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001063)  
the cost of borrowing low

**[01:26:03.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001064)  
while helping broaden

**[01:26:05.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001065)  
our investor base,

**[01:26:06.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001066)  
maintain access

**[01:26:07.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001067)  
to key international markets,

**[01:26:09.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001068)  
and support the continued development

**[01:26:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001069)  
of our local bond market.

**[01:26:16.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001070)  
With tax collections projected to grow

**[01:26:19.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001071)  
by an average of 9% annually

**[01:26:21.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001072)  
from 2026 to 2028,

**[01:26:24.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001073)  
total revenues is targeted

**[01:26:25.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001074)  
to reach 5.5 trillion pesos

**[01:26:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001075)  
by the end of the President's term.

**[01:26:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001076)  
By 2030,

**[01:26:32.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001077)  
we expect total revenues

**[01:26:34.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001078)  
to exceed 6 trillion.

**[01:26:36.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001079)  
This means our revenue agencies

**[01:26:39.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001080)  
will have to raise the bar

**[01:26:40.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001081)  
even further.

**[01:26:41.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001082)  
The BIR and BOC

**[01:26:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001083)  
will need to continue

**[01:26:44.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001084)  
improving efficiency,

**[01:26:46.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001085)  
strengthening compliance,

**[01:26:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001086)  
and leveraging technology

**[01:26:49.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001087)  
to sustain faster growth

**[01:26:51.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001088)  
in collections.

**[01:26:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001089)  
Coupled with stronger

**[01:26:54.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001090)  
collection efforts,

**[01:26:55.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001091)  
we are looking to pursue reforms

**[01:26:57.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001092)  
that will make our revenue system

**[01:26:59.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001093)  
more efficient,

**[01:27:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001094)  
equitable,

**[01:27:01.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001095)  
and sustainable.

**[01:27:03.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001096)  
We are promoting growth

**[01:27:05.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001097)  
revenue and equity

**[01:27:07.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001098)  
towards socio-economic stability,

**[01:27:10.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001099)  
sustainability,

**[01:27:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001100)  
or progress

**[01:27:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001101)  
through a comprehensive reform package

**[01:27:15.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001102)  
that provides targeted tax relief

**[01:27:18.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001103)  
while strengthening revenues

**[01:27:20.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001104)  
from products with social,

**[01:27:22.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001105)  
environmental,

**[01:27:23.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001106)  
and health costs.

**[01:27:26.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001107)  
We likewise expect more revenues

**[01:27:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001108)  
from the proposed

**[01:27:29.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001109)  
multinational minimum tax bill,

**[01:27:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001110)  
which will preserve our taxing rights

**[01:27:34.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001111)  
and ensure that taxes arising

**[01:27:36.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001112)  
from economic activity

**[01:27:37.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001113)  
in the Philippines

**[01:27:38.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001114)  
are collected here.

**[01:27:40.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001115)  
And the General Tax Amnesty Bill

**[01:27:44.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001116)  
mentioned by the President in his SONA

**[01:27:47.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001117)  
to encourage the settlement

**[01:27:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001118)  
of outstanding tax obligations,

**[01:27:50.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001119)  
improve compliance,

**[01:27:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001120)  
and bring more taxpayers

**[01:27:53.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001121)  
into the formal tax system.

**[01:27:56.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001122)  
Disbursements will grow moderately

**[01:28:00.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001123)  
from 2026 to 2028.

**[01:28:02.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001124)  
We must be more deliberate

**[01:28:04.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001125)  
about where we put our resources,

**[01:28:06.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001126)  
prioritizing investments

**[01:28:08.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001127)  
that strengthen productive capacity,

**[01:28:10.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001128)  
support long-term growth,

**[01:28:12.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001129)  
and deliver tangible benefits

**[01:28:14.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001130)  
to our people.

**[01:28:16.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001131)  
We must focus on programs

**[01:28:18.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001132)  
and investments that generate

**[01:28:19.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001133)  
the greatest economic

**[01:28:20.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001134)  
and social returns,

**[01:28:22.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001135)  
particularly in critical infrastructure,

**[01:28:25.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001136)  
health,

**[01:28:26.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001137)  
education,

**[01:28:27.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001138)  
and other areas

**[01:28:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001139)  
that support long-term growth.

**[01:28:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001140)  
As revenues grow faster

**[01:28:33.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001141)  
than disbursements,

**[01:28:35.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001142)  
we see a clear and sustained path

**[01:28:37.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001143)  
towards fiscal consolidation.

**[01:28:40.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001144)  
The deficit-to-GDP ratio

**[01:28:42.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001145)  
is projected to steadily decline

**[01:28:44.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001146)  
from 5.4% in 2026

**[01:28:47.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001147)  
to 3.5% by 2030,

**[01:28:51.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001148)  
putting us on a progressively stronger

**[01:28:53.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001149)  
fiscal footing through 2030.

**[01:28:57.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001150)  
This improving fiscal trajectory

**[01:28:59.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001151)  
is also reflected in our debt outlook.

**[01:29:02.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001152)  
From 2026 to 2030,

**[01:29:04.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001153)  
both national government

**[01:29:05.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001154)  
and general government debt ratios

**[01:29:07.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001155)  
are projected to decrease gradually.

**[01:29:11.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001156)  
National government debt

**[01:29:13.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001157)  
is projected to ease

**[01:29:14.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001158)  
from 65% to 63% in 2030,

**[01:29:19.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001159)  
while general government debt

**[01:29:21.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001160)  
is expected to decline

**[01:29:22.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001161)  
from 59% to 56%

**[01:29:25.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001162)  
over the same period.

**[01:29:28.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001163)  
As we look ahead,

**[01:29:30.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001164)  
the path we had laid out

**[01:29:31.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001165)  
is clear.

**[01:29:33.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001166)  
We assure the honorable members

**[01:29:34.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001167)  
of this Chamber

**[01:29:35.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001168)  
that the economic team

**[01:29:36.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001169)  
will continue to strengthen revenues,

**[01:29:38.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001170)  
manage expenditures responsibly,

**[01:29:41.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001171)  
reduce the deficit,

**[01:29:43.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001172)  
and keep our debt

**[01:29:44.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001173)  
on a sustainable path.

**[01:29:46.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001174)  
At the same time,

**[01:29:47.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001175)  
we are building fiscal buffers

**[01:29:48.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001176)  
that will give us room

**[01:29:50.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001177)  
to respond to future shocks

**[01:29:52.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001178)  
without compromising

**[01:29:53.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001179)  
our long-term fiscal sustainability.

**[01:29:56.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001180)  
We look forward

**[01:29:57.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001181)  
to working closely with Congress

**[01:29:59.080] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001182)  
to ensure that

**[01:30:00.000] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001183)  
ensure that the government lives within its means.

**[01:30:04.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001184)  
This means making difficult choices, prioritizing programs and investments with the greatest

**[01:30:10.380] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001185)  
impact, and ensuring that new spending commitments are matched by sustainable sources of funding.

**[01:30:19.420] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001186)  
Most importantly, let us continue to ensure that every peso of public fund works for the

**[01:30:25.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001187)  
Filipino people.

**[01:30:27.900] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001188)  
And our national budget can work harder when we invest in programs and projects that can

**[01:30:32.660] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001189)  
help shape our future.

**[01:30:36.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001190)  
The government must continue to address the immediate needs of our people, but we must

**[01:30:40.020] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001191)  
look beyond the present and invest in higher, multiplier programs and projects that create

**[01:30:47.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001192)  
lasting economic value, those that create jobs, raise incomes, and build a stronger

**[01:30:54.480] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001193)  
economy tomorrow.

**[01:30:57.220] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001194)  
Well-chosen

**[01:30:57.880] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001195)  
infrastructure, for example, does more than address today's needs.

**[01:31:03.160] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001196)  
It can generate economic activity, connect communities, attract private investment, and

**[01:31:09.220] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001197)  
deliver benefits for many years to come.

**[01:31:13.340] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001198)  
Let us work together to make the 2027 National Budget count and ensure that its benefits

**[01:31:19.180] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001199)  
ripple through generations.

**[01:31:22.280] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001200)  
This is the fiscal discipline we owe to the Filipino people.

**[01:31:26.000] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001201)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:31:27.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001202)  
Thank you very much, Secretary Frederick Go, for that presentation.

**[01:31:33.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001203)  
We will now move to the final presentation.

**[01:31:37.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001204)  
From the Department of Budget and Management, may now recognize the Honorable Secretary Kim

**[01:31:41.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001205)  
Robert C. de Leon.

**[01:31:43.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001206)  
To House Speaker Bodjie Dy III, the Majority Leader, the Minority Leader, House

**[01:31:52.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001207)  
Committee on Appropriations Chairperson Mikaela Angela Suansing, Vice Chairwoman Angela D.

**[01:31:56.160] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001208)  
Suansing, Vice Chairpersons of the Committee on Appropriations, Honorable Members of the

**[01:32:01.740] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001209)  
20th Congress, my fellow economic managers and colleagues in government, isang mapagpalang araw po sa ating lahat. As acting Secretary

**[01:32:11.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001210)  
of the Department of Budget and Management and Chair of the Development Budget Coordination

**[01:32:16.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001211)  
Committee, it is my honor to present the key dimensions and priorities of the Fiscal Year

**[01:32:23.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001212)  
2027 National Expenditure Program.

**[01:32:26.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001213)  
Thank you.

**[01:32:26.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001214)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:32:27.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001215)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:32:28.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001216)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:32:29.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001217)  
Thank you.

**[01:32:30.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001218)  
Thank you.

**[01:32:31.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001219)  
Let us now begin.

**[01:32:32.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001220)  
The National Expenditure Program.

**[01:32:33.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001221)  
Today, we are not simply presenting a 7.2 trillion peso spending plan.

**[01:32:34.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001222)  
We are presenting the results of months of careful review, difficult choices and rigorous

**[01:32:39.160] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001223)  
prioritization, determining what government must fund, what the country urgently needs,

**[01:32:46.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001224)  
what agencies are ready to implement and what our fiscal space can responsibly support.

**[01:32:52.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001225)  
With the theme of People-Centered Grants.

**[01:32:55.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001226)  
growth for an inclusive and resilient future the proposed national budget reflects our vision of

**[01:33:02.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001227)  
economic growth that is measured not only by stronger macroeconomic indicators but ultimately

**[01:33:09.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001228)  
by better lives for every filipino this budget is the product of the tireless work and dedication

**[01:33:16.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001229)  
of our fellow public servants at the dbm and across the departments and agencies of the

**[01:33:21.960] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001230)  
executive branch and let me emphasize that arriving at this budget was not a simple exercise

**[01:33:29.960] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001231)  
of adding up agency requests for fiscal year 2027 we received more than 11 trillion pesos in budget

**[01:33:40.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001232)  
proposals from different government agencies for possible inclusion in the nep but our total

**[01:33:48.440] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001233)  
expenditure program is 7.2 trillion

**[01:33:51.960] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001234)  
pesos clearly we could not accommodate every request we therefore had to carefully and

**[01:33:59.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001235)  
thoroughly deliberate on these proposals looking at their alignment with the president's priorities

**[01:34:06.120] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001236)  
in the philippine development plan implementation readiness absorptive capacity and past performance

**[01:34:14.280] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001237)  
project maturity expected outcomes and overall value to the filipino people

**[01:34:21.080] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001238)  
the nep is not the only project that has been implemented in the philippine development plan

**[01:34:21.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001239)  
implementation

**[01:34:23.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001240)  
the nep is not a compilation of everything government agencies asked for it is the result of choices and prioritization within the fiscal space available to government in simpler terms the nep is never a wish list it is a prioritized spending plan

**[01:34:40.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001241)  
guided by the clear direction of our president every effort was made to ensure that this budget

**[01:34:46.740] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001242)  
delivers real and meaningful benefits to every filipino

**[01:34:50.900] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001243)  
every region

**[01:34:51.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001244)  
and every community through to the spirit of our vision of bawat bayan maki kinabang

**[01:34:59.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001245)  
the proposed 7.2 trillion peso national budget is equivalent to 21.7 percent of gdp

**[01:35:08.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001246)  
and is 407 billion peso or 6 higher than the fiscal year 2026 budget the bulk of the increase

**[01:35:17.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001247)  
comes from mandatory requirements and existing fiscal obligations

**[01:35:21.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001248)  
including higher national tax allotment for our local government units,

**[01:35:26.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001249)  
the fourth tranche of salary adjustment for civilian government employees under EO No. 64,

**[01:35:34.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001250)  
the corresponding salary adjustments for our military and uniformed personnel,

**[01:35:38.800] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001251)  
and our other fiscal obligations of the government.

**[01:35:42.740] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001252)  
At the same time, we are making critical investments in people, infrastructure,

**[01:35:47.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001253)  
resilience, and public services to sustain economic growth.

**[01:35:53.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001254)  
The 6% increase does not mean that every agency or every program received a corresponding increase.

**[01:36:00.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001255)  
Every peso still had to compete for limited fiscal space,

**[01:36:04.660] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001256)  
and every major expenditure had to be justified.

**[01:36:09.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001257)  
Fundamentally, this budget is people-centered, inclusive, and resilient.

**[01:36:13.580] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001258)  
It invests in every Filipino,

**[01:36:16.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001259)  
extends opportunities,

**[01:36:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001260)  
contributes to every sector and every region,

**[01:36:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001261)  
and strengthens our nation's capacity to withstand future challenges

**[01:36:24.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001262)  
so that progress is not only sustained but shared by all.

**[01:36:28.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001263)  
Guided by this vision,

**[01:36:30.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001264)  
please allow me to present the dimensions and highlights of the Fiscal Year 2027 National Expenditure Program.

**[01:36:38.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001265)  
First and foremost,

**[01:36:40.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001266)  
this budget remains anchored in a disciplined, rules-based appropriations framework.

**[01:36:46.420] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001267)  
The proposed budget,

**[01:36:47.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001268)  
is composed of two main sources.

**[01:36:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001269)  
Programmed appropriations of 4.494 trillion pesos,

**[01:36:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001270)  
which fund the priority programs, projects, and operations of government.

**[01:37:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001271)  
And automatic appropriations of 2.706 trillion pesos,

**[01:37:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001272)  
which covers expenditures mandated under existing laws.

**[01:37:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001273)  
Among the major automatic appropriations are

**[01:37:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001274)  
1.32 trillion pesos,

**[01:37:16.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001275)  
1.32 trillion pesos,

**[01:37:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001276)  
for national tax allotment of local government units,

**[01:37:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001277)  
1.114 trillion pesos,

**[01:37:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001278)  
for interest payments,

**[01:37:27.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001279)  
104.2 billion pesos,

**[01:37:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001280)  
for the annual block grant of the Bangsamoro Autonomous Region in Musly, Mindanao,

**[01:37:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001281)  
and 89 billion for retirement and life insurance premiums of national government employees.

**[01:37:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001282)  
This demonstrates that a considerable portion

**[01:37:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001283)  
of the 7.2 trillion peso budget is already determined by laws,

**[01:37:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001284)  
formula, and existing obligations before government even begins deciding

**[01:37:58.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001285)  
which new or expanded programs to fund.

**[01:38:01.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001286)  
Meanwhile,

**[01:38:03.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001287)  
unprogrammed appropriations have been significantly reduced to just 112 billion pesos,

**[01:38:09.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001288)  
the lowest NEP proposal nominally since 2019,

**[01:38:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001289)  
and at only 1.56% of the total expenditure program,

**[01:38:21.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001290)  
likewise the lowest UA to total expenditure program ratio since 1991.

**[01:38:29.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001291)  
And for the benefit of our kababayans who are watching this briefing live,

**[01:38:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001292)  
please allow me to note that the inclusion of unprogrammed appropriations in the budget

**[01:38:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001293)  
does not mean that the amount is automatically available for release.

**[01:38:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001294)  
It is a standby spending authority for specifically identified purposes

**[01:38:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001295)  
that may be accessed only when the funding conditions prescribed by law are satisfied.

**[01:38:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001296)  
For fiscal year 2027,

**[01:38:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001297)  
the proposed UA has been deliberately narrowed primarily to finance,

**[01:39:03.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001298)  
partially the restoration of the fund balance previously remitted by the Philippine Deposit Insurance Corporation,

**[01:39:09.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001299)  
as well as to support foreign-assisted projects,

**[01:39:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001300)  
which are still currently being assessed and evaluated by the Investment Coordination Committee

**[01:39:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001301)  
and eventually for approval by the ED Council.

**[01:39:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001302)  
This reflects the administration's conscious policy choice

**[01:39:27.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001303)  
to put more expenditures under programmed appropriations

**[01:39:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001304)  
where funding is clearer and more predictable

**[01:39:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001305)  
while keeping standby appropriations to the minimum necessary.

**[01:39:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001306)  
The proposed fiscal year 2027

**[01:39:41.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001307)  
national budget was prepared on the basis of agencies' funding requirements

**[01:39:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001308)  
and with close consideration of their budget utilization and implementation capacity.

**[01:39:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001309)  
As of June 30, 2026,

**[01:39:58.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001310)  
total available appropriations had reached P7.54 trillion,

**[01:40:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001311)  
of which P6.63 trillion had already been released

**[01:40:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001312)  
as allotments to various agencies,

**[01:40:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001313)  
including P546.79 billion from continuing appropriations from fiscal year 2025.

**[01:40:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001314)  
Of these allotments,

**[01:40:24.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001315)  
agencies had already obligated P3.29 trillion or 49.5%,

**[01:40:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001316)  
comprising P3.13 trillion from fiscal year 2026 appropriations

**[01:40:38.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001317)  
and P158.58 billion from fiscal year 2025 continuing appropriations.

**[01:40:45.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001318)  
In terms of disbursements,

**[01:40:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001319)  
81.9% of the reported obligations have been paid.

**[01:40:53.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001320)  
Meanwhile, unreleased appropriations amounted to P820.21 billion

**[01:41:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001321)  
as of June 30 from the fiscal year 2026 GAA

**[01:41:05.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001322)  
and P89.7 billion

**[01:41:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001323)  
from fiscal year 2025 continuing appropriations.

**[01:41:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001324)  
These data have guided our budget preparation process

**[01:41:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001325)  
and reinforced the need to align future allocations

**[01:41:18.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001326)  
with agencies' implementation capacity.

**[01:41:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001327)  
This reflects our commitment to ensuring that budgets

**[01:41:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001328)  
are not only approved and released,

**[01:41:27.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001329)  
but effectively implemented,

**[01:41:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001330)  
because every peso delivers its true value

**[01:41:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001331)  
only when it is translated into timely,

**[01:41:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001332)  
measurable, and meaningful outcomes for the Filipino people.

**[01:41:41.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001333)  
By expense class,

**[01:41:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001334)  
the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget

**[01:41:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001335)  
reflects a disciplined spending strategy

**[01:41:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001336)  
to sustain government operations,

**[01:41:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001337)  
invest in long-term development,

**[01:41:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001338)  
and fulfill our fiscal obligations.

**[01:41:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001339)  
Maintenance and other operating expenses, or MOOE,

**[01:42:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001340)  
will receive P2.944 trillion.

**[01:42:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001341)  
In addition,

**[01:42:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001342)  
the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget

**[01:42:09.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001343)  
reflects a 1.2% decrease from the fiscal year 2027 GAA

**[01:42:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001344)  
despite the 6% overall increase in the national budget.

**[01:42:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001345)  
In absolute terms,

**[01:42:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001346)  
MOOE actually decreases by P36.2 billion.

**[01:42:24.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001347)  
This shows that growth of the budget is not driven

**[01:42:27.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001348)  
by a broad expansion in operating expenditures.

**[01:42:32.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001349)  
We have allocated P1.982 trillion

**[01:42:35.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001350)  
for personnel services,

**[01:42:38.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001351)  
an increase of P138.2 billion,

**[01:42:41.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001352)  
or 7.5%,

**[01:42:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001353)  
to support the public servants who deliver education,

**[01:42:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001354)  
health care, public safety, national defense,

**[01:42:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001355)  
and other essential services.

**[01:42:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001356)  
This also reflects salary adjustments

**[01:42:55.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001357)  
and other personnel requirements

**[01:42:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001358)  
already committed by the government.

**[01:42:59.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001359)  
Notably, PS and MOOE remain fully covered

**[01:43:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001360)  
by our projected revenues,

**[01:43:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001361)  
reinforcing our commitment to fiscal discipline.

**[01:43:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001362)  
Meanwhile, capital outlays increased to P1.16 trillion,

**[01:43:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001363)  
up by P140.8 billion, or 13.8%,

**[01:43:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001364)  
allowing government to invest in infrastructure,

**[01:43:27.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001365)  
equipment, facilities, and other assets

**[01:43:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001366)  
with long-term economic and social value.

**[01:43:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001367)  
Financial expenses amount to P1.114 trillion,

**[01:43:38.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001368)  
up by P164.3 billion, or 17.3%,

**[01:43:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001369)  
primarily reflecting interest payments.

**[01:43:47.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001370)  
Allow me to briefly note the context

**[01:43:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001371)  
behind the increase in interest payments.

**[01:43:51.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001372)  
A higher interest bill does not by itself mean

**[01:43:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001373)  
that our debt has become unmanageable.

**[01:43:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001374)  
Our debt remains manageable,

**[01:43:59.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001375)  
as previously mentioned by the Secretary of Finance,

**[01:44:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001376)  
and debt servicing is explicitly provided for in the budget.

**[01:44:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001377)  
At the same time, our medium-term fiscal program

**[01:44:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001378)  
is designed to gradually narrow the deficit

**[01:44:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001379)  
while protecting growth-enhancing

**[01:44:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001380)  
and socially important expenditures.

**[01:44:18.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001381)  
Our approach is therefore not to sacrifice growth

**[01:44:21.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001382)  
simply to reduce the deficit overnight.

**[01:44:24.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001383)  
We are pursuing a strategic and gradual fiscal consolidation path

**[01:44:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001384)  
that allows us to honor our obligations,

**[01:44:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001385)  
maintain fiscal credibility,

**[01:44:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001386)  
and continue investing in our people and our economy.

**[01:44:37.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001387)  
The proposed national budget will be allocated across five sectors,

**[01:44:42.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001388)  
with the largest share, P34.1%,

**[01:44:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001389)  
or P2.456 trillion going to social services.

**[01:44:51.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001390)  
This finances investments that Filipinos encounter directly

**[01:44:55.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001391)  
in their everyday lives.

**[01:44:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001392)  
Education, health, social protection,

**[01:45:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001393)  
employment, housing, and other essential services.

**[01:45:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001394)  
Economic services will receive 25.5%,

**[01:45:08.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001395)  
or P1.833 trillion,

**[01:45:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001396)  
supporting infrastructure, agriculture,

**[01:45:14.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001397)  
transportation, water resources,

**[01:45:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001398)  
digital connectivity, and other productivity-enhancing investments.

**[01:45:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001399)  
General public services will receive 18.3%,

**[01:45:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001400)  
or P1.317 trillion,

**[01:45:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001401)  
while defense will receive 6.3%,

**[01:45:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001402)  
or P452.4 billion,

**[01:45:37.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001403)  
as we strengthen our capacity to protect our territory,

**[01:45:41.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001404)  
our communities, and our people.

**[01:45:44.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001405)  
Meanwhile, 15.9%, or P1.143 trillion,

**[01:45:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001406)  
is allocated for debt burden,

**[01:45:51.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001407)  
covering interest payments and net lending

**[01:45:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001408)  
to government corporations.

**[01:45:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001409)  
Again, debt service should be viewed in its proper context.

**[01:46:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001410)  
Meeting our obligations protects the government's creditworthiness,

**[01:46:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001411)  
preserves investor confidence,

**[01:46:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001412)  
and helps keep financing available for future public investments.

**[01:46:12.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001413)  
The important point is balance.

**[01:46:14.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001414)  
We honor what we owe while continuing to invest

**[01:46:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001415)  
in what our people need.

**[01:46:20.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001416)  
By recipient unit,

**[01:46:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001417)  
national government agencies will receive the largest share

**[01:46:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001418)  
at P4.332 trillion,

**[01:46:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001419)  
enabling them to implement priority programs and projects nationwide.

**[01:46:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001420)  
But even as NGAs receive the largest nominal share,

**[01:46:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001421)  
their allocation grows by only 4.5%,

**[01:46:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001422)  
below the overall 6% growth of the national budget,

**[01:46:44.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001423)  
again reflecting restraint in national government spending.

**[01:46:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001424)  
Local government units will receive P1.533 trillion,

**[01:46:53.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001425)  
recognizing their critical role in delivering frontline services

**[01:46:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001426)  
and responding to the needs of local communities.

**[01:47:01.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001427)  
A major component is the P1.32 trillion NTA,

**[01:47:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001428)  
compared with around P1.191 trillion this year,

**[01:47:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001429)  
an increase of roughly P129.3 billion

**[01:47:16.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001430)  
flowing directly to our local government units.

**[01:47:21.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001431)  
We have also provided P220.5 billion for GOCCs

**[01:47:26.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001432)  
and P1.14 trillion for interest payments.

**[01:47:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001433)  
This distribution shows that the budget is not concentrated

**[01:47:35.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001434)  
solely in the national bureaucracy.

**[01:47:37.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001435)  
A substantial and growing amount is being transferred to the local level

**[01:47:42.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001436)  
where many government services are actually delivered to our citizens.

**[01:47:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001437)  
This brings us to another important dimension

**[01:47:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001438)  
of the budget, where the money will go.

**[01:47:53.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001439)  
Of the proposed fiscal year 2027 national budget,

**[01:47:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001440)  
P4.603 trillion or 63.9% will be directly allocated

**[01:48:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001441)  
to the regions to support programs and projects

**[01:48:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001442)  
responsive to local development priorities.

**[01:48:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001443)  
Of this amount, National Capital Region will receive P1.112 trillion,

**[01:48:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001444)  
Luzon at P1.659 trillion,

**[01:48:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001445)  
Visayas at P744.8 billion,

**[01:48:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001446)  
and Mindanao at P1.088 trillion.

**[01:48:27.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001447)  
The remaining 36.1% or P2.597 trillion

**[01:48:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001448)  
constitutes the non-regionalized budget.

**[01:48:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001449)  
This consists of nationwide allocations

**[01:48:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001450)  
amounting to P1.983 trillion or 27.5%

**[01:48:45.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001451)  
and central office allocations totaling

**[01:48:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001452)  
to P213.8 billion or 8.5%.

**[01:48:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001453)  
But nationwide should not be mistaken for Metro Manila.

**[01:48:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001454)  
These include resources that will eventually benefit regions

**[01:49:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001455)  
and beneficiaries across the country

**[01:49:03.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001456)  
but cannot yet be geographically assigned

**[01:49:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001457)  
at the time the NEP is prepared.

**[01:49:08.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001458)  
The broader direction is that the majority of the budget

**[01:49:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001459)  
is intended to reach our regions and communities.

**[01:49:14.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001460)  
This is what we mean when we say

**[01:49:16.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001461)  
Bawat Bayan Makikinabang.

**[01:49:20.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001462)  
With the overall framework and dimensions of the budget laid out,

**[01:49:24.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001463)  
let us now look more closely at where some of our biggest investments will go.

**[01:49:29.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001464)  
The Department of Education will receive the highest allocation

**[01:49:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001465)  
at P975.96 billion,

**[01:49:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001466)  
affirming the government's enduring commitment

**[01:49:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001467)  
to investing in our people.

**[01:49:41.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001468)  
But beyond basic education,

**[01:49:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001469)  
the proposed budget allocates

**[01:49:45.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001470)  
P176.5 billion for higher education

**[01:49:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001471)  
to ensure that more Filipinos have access to quality

**[01:49:53.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001472)  
and affordable tertiary education.

**[01:49:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001473)  
Altogether, the proposed investment in basic and higher education

**[01:50:01.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001474)  
amounts to P1.326 trillion,

**[01:50:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001475)  
equivalent to nearly 4% of GDP,

**[01:50:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001476)  
effectively reaching the lower bound

**[01:50:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001477)  
of the UNESCO Education 2030 benchmark

**[01:50:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001478)  
of 4-6%.

**[01:50:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001479)  
Within these totals are very concrete investments.

**[01:50:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001480)  
We are proposing P12.09 billion

**[01:50:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001481)  
for the expanded career progression system

**[01:50:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001482)  
for public school teachers,

**[01:50:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001483)  
nearly double the fiscal year 2026 level,

**[01:50:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001484)  
to support the reclassification

**[01:50:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001485)  
and career advancement of our teachers.

**[01:50:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001486)  
This reflects an increase of P5.94 billion

**[01:50:44.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001487)  
or 96.6% from the fiscal year 2026 level.

**[01:50:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001488)  
We are providing P10.36 billion

**[01:50:53.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001489)  
for the teaching allowance,

**[01:50:55.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001490)  
sustaining the P10,000 allowance provided

**[01:50:59.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001491)  
under the Kabalikat sa Pagtuturo Act.

**[01:51:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001492)  
For tertiary education,

**[01:51:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001493)  
P61.1 billion is provided

**[01:51:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001494)  
for the universal access

**[01:51:09.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001495)  
to quality tertiary education

**[01:51:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001496)  
across SUCs,

**[01:51:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001497)  
CHED, and TESDA,

**[01:51:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001498)  
supporting free higher education,

**[01:51:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001499)  
tertiary education subsidies,

**[01:51:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001500)  
and free technical vocational education.

**[01:51:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001501)  
More than maintaining institutions,

**[01:51:26.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001502)  
these investments focus more

**[01:51:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001503)  
on addressing learning losses,

**[01:51:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001504)  
supporting our teachers,

**[01:51:32.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001505)  
keeping young Filipinos in school,

**[01:51:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001506)  
improving skills,

**[01:51:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001507)  
and building the human capital

**[01:51:38.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001508)  
that will determine our country's competitiveness

**[01:51:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001509)  
for decades to come.

**[01:51:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001510)  
Now, let's look more closely

**[01:51:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001511)  
at where the money goes

**[01:51:47.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001512)  
and which priorities receive

**[01:51:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001513)  
the largest departmental allocations.

**[01:51:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001514)  
The 10 departments with the largest allocations

**[01:51:55.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001515)  
account for 51.4%

**[01:51:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001516)  
of the proposed national budget,

**[01:52:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001517)  
reflecting the administration's major priorities

**[01:52:03.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001518)  
for fiscal year 2027.

**[01:52:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001519)  
At the top, like I mentioned,

**[01:52:08.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001520)  
is the Department of Education

**[01:52:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001521)  
with P975.96 billion,

**[01:52:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001522)  
followed by the Department of Public Works

**[01:52:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001523)  
and Highways with 644 billion.

**[01:52:18.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001524)  
Substantial investments

**[01:52:20.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001525)  
are likewise proposed for health,

**[01:52:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001526)  
interior and local government,

**[01:52:24.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001527)  
national defense,

**[01:52:26.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001528)  
transportation, agriculture,

**[01:52:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001529)  
social welfare and development,

**[01:52:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001530)  
higher education,

**[01:52:32.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001531)  
and the judiciary.

**[01:52:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001532)  
These rankings show that the government

**[01:52:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001533)  
is putting its greatest resources

**[01:52:38.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001534)  
in our people,

**[01:52:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001535)  
our infrastructure,

**[01:52:41.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001536)  
our health and safety,

**[01:52:42.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001537)  
our food security,

**[01:52:44.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001538)  
our mobility,

**[01:52:45.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001539)  
and the institutions that protect

**[01:52:47.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001540)  
and serve our citizens.

**[01:52:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001541)  
On infrastructure,

**[01:52:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001542)  
let me emphasize that infrastructure

**[01:52:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001543)  
is more than just the DPWH budget.

**[01:52:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001544)  
Under the Build Better More

**[01:52:59.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001545)  
infrastructure program,

**[01:53:01.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001546)  
the government is proposing

**[01:53:03.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001547)  
1.467 trillion,

**[01:53:05.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001548)  
equivalent to 4.4% of GDP

**[01:53:09.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001549)  
for infrastructure in 2027.

**[01:53:12.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001550)  
This is 178 billion

**[01:53:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001551)  
or 13.8% higher

**[01:53:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001552)  
than the fiscal year 2026

**[01:53:20.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001553)  
GAA infrastructure level.

**[01:53:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001554)  
These investments are spread across

**[01:53:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001555)  
DPWH, transportation,

**[01:53:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001556)  
agriculture, education,

**[01:53:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001557)  
defense, digital infrastructure,

**[01:53:32.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001558)  
health and other agencies.

**[01:53:35.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001559)  
For DPWH specifically,

**[01:53:37.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001560)  
it's proposed 643.95 billion allocation

**[01:53:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001561)  
is 21.29% higher

**[01:53:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001562)  
than its fiscal year 2026 GAA level,

**[01:53:51.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001563)  
driven in part by ongoing locally funded

**[01:53:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001564)  
and foreign-assisted

**[01:53:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001565)  
infrastructure flagship projects.

**[01:53:58.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001566)  
Among the major projects

**[01:54:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001567)  
are the following.

**[01:54:01.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001568)  
The Laguna Lakeshore Road Network project

**[01:54:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001569)  
at 35.21 billion,

**[01:54:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001570)  
Bataan-Cavite Interlink Bridge project

**[01:54:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001571)  
at 22.49 billion,

**[01:54:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001572)  
Davao City Bypass Construction Project

**[01:54:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001573)  
Package 1 at 11.96 billion,

**[01:54:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001574)  
Cebu-Mactan Bridge and Coastal Road

**[01:54:21.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001575)  
Construction Project at 8.57 billion,

**[01:54:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001576)  
and the Pasig-Marikina River Channel

**[01:54:27.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001577)  
Improvement Project Phase 4

**[01:54:30.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001578)  
at 8.54 billion.

**[01:54:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001579)  
For transportation,

**[01:54:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001580)  
we are proposing 300.96 billion

**[01:54:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001581)  
for the DOTR and its attached agencies,

**[01:54:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001582)  
supporting major investments

**[01:54:45.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001583)  
that will improve mobility,

**[01:54:47.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001584)  
strengthen connectivity,

**[01:54:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001585)  
and make the movement of people and goods

**[01:54:51.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001586)  
faster and more efficient across the country.

**[01:54:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001587)  
Among the priority projects

**[01:54:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001588)  
are the North-South Commuter Railway System

**[01:54:59.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001589)  
with 123.84 billion,

**[01:55:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001590)  
the Metro Manila Subway Project Phase 1

**[01:55:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001591)  
with 67.44 billion,

**[01:55:08.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001592)  
and the Metro Rail Transit Line 3 Rehabilitation Project

**[01:55:13.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001593)  
with 1.94 billion.

**[01:55:18.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001594)  
We are also providing 179.49 million

**[01:55:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001595)  
for the Cebu Bus Rapid Transit Project

**[01:55:26.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001596)  
and 15.08 billion

**[01:55:29.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001597)  
for the Davao Public Transportation

**[01:55:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001598)  
Modernization Project,

**[01:55:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001599)  
expanding investments in better and more reliable

**[01:55:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001600)  
public transportation,

**[01:55:38.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001601)  
beyond Metro Manila.

**[01:55:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001602)  
We are likewise investing in our

**[01:55:42.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001603)  
Gateways and Logistics Network
