**[01:55:44.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001604)  
with 8.99 billion

**[01:55:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001605)  
for the new Cebu International Container Port

**[01:55:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001606)  
and 1.41 billion

**[01:55:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001607)  
for the new Dumaguete Airport Development Project.

**[01:55:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001608)  
More than investing in railways,

**[01:55:58.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001609)  
public transportation systems,

**[01:56:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001610)  
ports or airports,

**[01:56:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001611)  
these also show that investing

**[01:56:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001612)  
in shorter travel times,

**[01:56:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001613)  
lower logistics costs,

**[01:56:08.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001614)  
stronger regional connectivity

**[01:56:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001615)  
and greater access to jobs

**[01:56:12.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001616)  
and economic opportunities for our people

**[01:56:14.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001617)  
are important priorities

**[01:56:16.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001618)  
of this administration.

**[01:56:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001619)  
For agriculture and food security,

**[01:56:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001620)  
261.7 billion is proposed

**[01:56:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001621)  
across the agriculture sector,

**[01:56:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001622)  
supporting programs that increase productivity,

**[01:56:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001623)  
strengthen food security,

**[01:56:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001624)  
and improve the income

**[01:56:35.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001625)  
of our farmers and fisher folks.

**[01:56:38.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001626)  
For social protection,

**[01:56:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001627)  
DSWD will receive 241.6 billion.

**[01:56:45.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001628)  
This includes 99.08 billion

**[01:56:49.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001629)  
for the Pantawid Pamilyang Pilipino Program,

**[01:56:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001630)  
covering health, education,

**[01:56:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001631)  
nutrition and rice subsidies

**[01:56:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001632)  
for qualified households.

**[01:57:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001633)  
For national defense,

**[01:57:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001634)  
328.8 billion is proposed,

**[01:57:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001635)  
including 50 billion

**[01:57:08.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001636)  
for the revised AFP Modernization Program,

**[01:57:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001637)  
10 billion higher than the 2026 level.

**[01:57:15.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001638)  
The Philippine Coast Guard

**[01:57:16.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001639)  
will likewise receive 45.51 billion,

**[01:57:20.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001640)  
while 73.67 billion

**[01:57:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001641)  
is proposed for the subsistence allowance

**[01:57:26.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001642)  
of military and uniform personnel

**[01:57:29.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001643)  
across concerned agencies.

**[01:57:32.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001644)  
We have likewise included

**[01:57:34.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001645)  
86.27 billion

**[01:57:37.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001646)  
for the judiciary

**[01:57:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001647)  
to support the efficient

**[01:57:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001648)  
administration of justice

**[01:57:42.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001649)  
and improve access

**[01:57:44.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001650)  
to judicial services.

**[01:57:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001651)  
In keeping with the constitutional principle

**[01:57:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001652)  
of judicial independence

**[01:57:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001653)  
and the provisions

**[01:57:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001654)  
of the Judiciary Fiscal Autonomy Act,

**[01:57:55.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001655)  
the Supreme Court's

**[01:57:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001656)  
original budget proposal

**[01:57:58.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001657)  
has been incorporated

**[01:58:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001658)  
in the Fiscal Year 2027

**[01:58:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001659)  
National Expenditure Program.

**[01:58:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001660)  
This reflects

**[01:58:06.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001661)  
the judiciary's fiscal autonomy

**[01:58:08.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001662)  
while ensuring that it remains

**[01:58:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001663)  
well equipped to carry out

**[01:58:12.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001664)  
its constitutional mandate.

**[01:58:14.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001665)  
We are also proposing

**[01:58:16.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001666)  
45.67 billion

**[01:58:18.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001667)  
for the National Disaster

**[01:58:20.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001668)  
Risk Reduction and Management Fund,

**[01:58:22.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001669)  
up by 14.68%

**[01:58:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001670)  
from the Fiscal Year 2026 GAA.

**[01:58:29.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001671)  
This includes 14.67 billion

**[01:58:32.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001672)  
for the Disaster Risk Reduction and Management Fund,

**[01:58:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001673)  
for the Disaster Rehabilitation

**[01:58:35.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001674)  
and Reconstruction Assistance Program

**[01:58:37.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001675)  
for LGUs,

**[01:58:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001676)  
and 1 billion

**[01:58:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001677)  
for the People's Survival Fund.

**[01:58:42.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001678)  
These numbers demonstrate

**[01:58:44.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001679)  
the overall design of this budget.

**[01:58:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001680)  
Protect the vulnerable,

**[01:58:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001681)  
invest in people,

**[01:58:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001682)  
strengthen food and health security,

**[01:58:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001683)  
build productive infrastructure,

**[01:58:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001684)  
support our regions,

**[01:58:56.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001685)  
protect the country,

**[01:58:58.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001686)  
and prepare for future shocks.

**[01:59:00.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001687)  
Finally,

**[01:59:02.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001688)  
in line with the Administration's vision

**[01:59:04.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001689)  
of ensuring that

**[01:59:05.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001690)  
Bawat Bayan Makikinabang,

**[01:59:07.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001691)  
the proposed 2027 National Budget

**[01:59:10.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001692)  
also empowers every region

**[01:59:12.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001693)  
and strengthens local governments

**[01:59:14.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001694)  
to support balanced regional development

**[01:59:17.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001695)  
across the country.

**[01:59:19.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001696)  
As discussed earlier,

**[01:59:20.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001697)  
we are providing

**[01:59:21.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001698)  
1.32 trillion

**[01:59:23.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001699)  
for the National Tax Allotment,

**[01:59:25.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001700)  
ensuring that local government units

**[01:59:28.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001701)  
have the resources

**[01:59:29.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001702)  
to effectively deliver,

**[01:59:31.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001703)  
devolve services,

**[01:59:33.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001704)  
and 115.62 billion pesos

**[01:59:36.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001705)  
to help sustain peace,

**[01:59:39.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001706)  
strengthen self-governance,

**[01:59:41.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001707)  
and support long-term development

**[01:59:43.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001708)  
in the Bangsamoro Autonomous Region

**[01:59:46.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001709)  
in Muslim Mindanao.

**[01:59:48.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001710)  
We are likewise allocating

**[01:59:50.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001711)  
58.53 billion

**[01:59:52.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001712)  
for the Local Government Support Fund,

**[01:59:54.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001713)  
including financial assistance to LGUs,

**[01:59:57.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001714)  
the Growth Equity Fund,

**[02:00:00.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001715)  
and the Barangay Development Program, enabling local governments to implement priority infrastructure,

**[02:00:05.980] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001716)  
social services, and community development projects that directly benefit their constituents.

**[02:00:13.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001717)  
These investments recognize that by empowering our local governments and investing in every region,

**[02:00:20.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001718)  
we ensure that growth is more inclusive, opportunities are more widely shared, and no community is left behind.

**[02:00:30.740] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001719)  
These are just some of the key highlights of our proposed fiscal year 2027 National Budget.

**[02:00:37.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001720)  
We have also prepared a short video presentation to provide an overview of the administration's priority programs, activities, and projects,

**[02:00:47.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001721)  
all crafted and funded for the Filipino people.

**[02:00:51.120] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001722)  
Before I end my presentation, allow me to echo the President's call during the latest State of the Nation Address.

**[02:00:57.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001723)  
Sa nalalabing dalawang taon ng administrasyon, tatapusin natin ang mga nasimulan at tutuparin ang ating pangako sa sambayanan.

**[02:01:08.300] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001724)  
That instruction is also reflected in how we crafted this budget.

**[02:01:13.200] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001725)  
We made sure that this 7.2 trillion budget only funds what is necessary, what is ready,

**[02:01:20.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001726)  
and what can deliver the greatest benefit to our people.

**[02:01:24.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001727)  
Every peso that we propose to spend must have a clear purpose, a sound basis, and an intended result for the Filipino people.

**[02:01:33.320] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001728)  
The DBM remains committed to funding the government's priorities and to ensuring that this budget delivers on its promises

**[02:01:40.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001729)  
through utmost transparency, accountability, fiscal discipline, and integrity.

**[02:01:47.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001730)  
Because for us, DBM now stands for...

**[02:01:50.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001731)  
Dapat budget ay makatao, dapat ang budget ay maaasahan, dapat ang budget ay malinis.

**[02:01:58.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001732)  
Dahil ang budget na makatao, maaasahan, at malinis ay isang budget na mapapakinabangan ng bawat mamamayan, pati na ng susunod na henerasyon.

**[02:02:09.280] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-001733)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[02:03:38.140] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001753)

**[02:03:42.240] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001754)

**[02:03:46.680] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001755)  
Our overarching mission and all our conscious efforts,

**[02:03:53.820] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001757)

**[02:04:00.000] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001758)  
Ang pinakamatibay na pundasyon ng isang bansa ay isang mamamayang may edukasyon.

**[02:04:06.620] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001759)

**[02:04:12.220] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001760)

**[02:04:24.060] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001762)

**[02:04:29.160] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001763)

**[02:04:32.360] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001764)

**[02:04:45.700] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001766)

**[02:04:50.280] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001767)

**[02:04:59.540] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001768)  
Sapagkat kapag ang isang bata ay nabigyan ng pagkakataong matuto,

**[02:05:04.340] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001769)

**[02:05:11.460] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001770)  
PAS has given me the chance to pursue my dreams, the confidence to take whatever opportunity that life gives me,

**[02:05:19.760] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001771)  
and the capacity for me to become an inspiration

**[02:05:23.500] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001772)  
and to become someone who can now contribute to the society.

**[02:05:27.860] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001773)  
Ang malusog na mamamayan ang tunay na lakas ng isang bansa.

**[02:05:32.160] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001774)  
Kaya mas pinalalakas ng panukalang budget

**[02:05:34.860] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001775)  
ang mga servisyong pangkalusugan na mas malapit sa bawat komunidad.

**[02:05:40.980] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001776)  
Mas mataas na pondo ang ilalaan para sa mga DOH regional hospitals

**[02:05:45.420] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001777)  
at iba pang health facilities.

**[02:05:47.760] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001778)  
Malalawakin din ang Zero Balance Billing ng PhilHealth

**[02:05:51.700] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001779)  
mula anim na LGU hospitals na yung taon

**[02:05:54.760] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001780)  
tungo sa 352 LGU hospitals sa 2027.

**[02:06:00.080] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001781)  
Mas payigin din ang suporta sa mahigit na 26,000 healthcare workers

**[02:06:05.040] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001782)  
at dinoble rin ang pondo para sa Cancer Assistance Fund

**[02:06:08.860] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001783)  
upang mas marami pang pasyente ang makatanggap ng kinakailangang tulong.

**[02:06:13.780] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001784)  
Dahil ang dekalidad na servisyong pangkalusugan

**[02:06:16.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001785)  
ay hindi dapat maging pribilehyo.

**[02:06:20.000] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001786)  
Ito ay dapat maabot ng bawat Pilipino.

**[02:06:23.780] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001787)  
Nagdadalaw ang isip kung tutuloy ba o hindi

**[02:06:26.060] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001788)  
kasi pambaya din ang iniisip namin.

**[02:06:29.120] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001789)  
Nung nalaman namin na wala kaming babaya, masaya kami.

Simula noong 2024 hanggang ngayon,

**[02:06:38.280] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001791)

**[02:06:40.300] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001792)  
Ngayon malakas ang loob namin na dali sa airfat

**[02:06:42.220] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001793)  
kasi alam namin wala kaming babaya sa hospital.

**[02:06:45.380] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001794)  
Ang tunay na kaunlaran,

**[02:06:46.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001795)  
ay walang naiiwan.

**[02:06:47.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001796)  
Kaya patuloy na pinalalakas ng pamahalaan

**[02:06:50.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001797)  
ang mga programang nagbibigay ng proteksyon at pag-asa

**[02:06:54.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001798)  
sa mga higit na nangangailangan.

**[02:06:57.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001799)  
Milyon-milyong pamilya ang patuloy na makatatanggap ng suporta

**[02:07:01.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001800)  
sa ilalim ng Pantawid Pamilyang Pilipino Program.

**[02:07:05.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001801)  
Mas paiigtingin ang food assistance at electronic voucher programs

**[02:07:09.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001802)  
laban sa gutom at malnutrisyon.

**[02:07:11.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001803)  
Mas magiging handa rin ang limandaang munisipalidad

**[02:07:14.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001804)  
sa epekto ng COVID-19.

**[02:07:16.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001805)  
At mga kalamidan sa pamamagitan ng Philippine Community Resilience Project.

**[02:07:21.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001806)  
Kasabay nito,

**[02:07:22.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001807)  
magpapatuloy ang buwanang social pension

**[02:07:25.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001808)  
para sa apat na milyong indigent senior citizens

**[02:07:28.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001809)  
at ang tulong para sa ating mga OFW sa oras ng pangangailangan.

**[02:07:33.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001810)  
Sapagkat ang isang pamahalaang may malasakit

**[02:07:36.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001811)  
ay hindi lamang tumutulong sa panahon ng pangangailangan.

**[02:07:39.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001812)  
Nagbibigay ito ng pag-asa upang mas maging matatag ang bawat Pilipino.

**[02:07:45.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001813)  
Napakalaking tulong po sa amin

**[02:07:47.940] Government Budget Presentation Narrator** (asr-001814)

**[02:15:52.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001901)  
Secretary, your medium term fiscal,

**[02:15:56.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001902)  
your fiscal program shows a deficit declining from 5.4% of GDP in 2026 to 3.5% by 2030.

**[02:16:10.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001903)  
But over the same period, revenues actually fall from 15.8% to 15.6% of GDP,

**[02:16:21.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001904)  
while disbursements fall from 21.2% to 19.1% of GDP.

**[02:16:23.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001905)  
While disbursements fall from 21.2% to 19.1% of GDP,

**[02:16:24.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001906)  
while disbursements fall from 21.2% to 19.1% of GDP,

**[02:16:25.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001907)  
21.2%

**[02:16:27.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001908)  
to 19.1%.

**[02:16:30.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001909)  
Is it therefore correct that

**[02:16:31.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001910)  
the improvement in the deficit comes

**[02:16:34.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001911)  
essentially from compressing government

**[02:16:38.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001912)  
spending and not from improving revenue effort?

**[02:16:43.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001913)

**[02:16:48.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001914)  
lumiliit ang ating collection pero may increase, may

**[02:16:50.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001915)  
ah decline tayo sa ah GDP. Pero ang

**[02:16:53.940] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001916)  
Declined tayo sa GDP. Pero ang nakikita natin, parang iniipit lang natin. Hindi nangungulik na tayo para maka-create ng fiscal space. Is this correct, Secretary?

**[02:17:12.960] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001917)  
Thank you, Chairman of the Minority Floor Leader. Madam Chair, I have to answer this in three ways. The first one is revenues are not dropping. Revenues will continue to grow.

**[02:17:30.780] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001918)  
As a matter of fact, we are projecting revenues to grow at an average of about 9 plus percent. What was pointed out is the percentage to GDP.

**[02:17:42.960] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001919)  
But actually, collections are growing. In fact, they'll grow by 9.5 percent. And yes, I think the idea, that's the first part of the answer.

**[02:17:52.860] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001920)  
The second part of the answer, Madam Chair, is that the government has been spending heavily on infrastructure for the last many years. And we continue to do so.

**[02:18:08.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001921)  
I think the idea is that all these investments in infrastructure will continue to grow.

**[02:18:12.960] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001922)  
And in other forms of investments, whether they're schools, hospitals, will eventually have to pay off. And the country cannot also be in a perpetual state of spending.

**[02:18:31.320] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001923)  
So, as a percentage of GDP, expenditures...

**[02:18:42.960] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001924)  
It is expected to slow down as a percentage of GDP. But in absolute numbers, it also continues to grow.

**[02:18:55.260] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001925)  
So, expenditures will still grow by 5 to 6 percent every year. So, actually, we're not shrinking revenues and we're not shrinking expenditure, just as a percentage of GDP.

**[02:19:07.720] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-001926)  
Yeah, that's how I would answer the question.

**[02:19:12.960] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001927)  
Is it correct that we're compressing to create the fiscal space?

**[02:19:17.360] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001928)  
We are reducing the fiscal deficit.

**[02:19:20.580] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001929)  
The goal is to reduce the fiscal deficit over time.

**[02:19:24.260] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001930)  
So, when we came into the administration, the fiscal deficit...

**[02:19:42.960] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001931)  
When we came into the office, the fiscal deficit was 7.3 percent.

**[02:19:53.720] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001932)  
Then we worked it down to 6.2 percent, 5.7 percent, 5.6 percent. Now, we're targeting 5.45 percent.

**[02:20:03.080] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001933)  
And we want to keep bringing that down.

**[02:20:06.060] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001934)  
That is part of our commitment to the international community, to the credit rating.

**[02:20:12.960] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001935)  
That is part of our commitment to the credit rating agencies that we will practice fiscal consolidation, which means we will bring down our fiscal deficit over time.

**[02:20:27.400] Minority Leader Marcelino C. Libanan** (asr-001936)

**[02:55:24.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002610)

**[02:55:34.360] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002611)

**[02:55:42.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002612)  
na ginamit po ng DBM in evaluating the public works proposal submitted by the DPWH.

**[02:55:48.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002613)

**[02:55:54.540] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002614)  
Representative Salvador Pleyto.

during the time of Secretary Bautista,

**[02:57:47.420] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002634)

**[02:58:02.140] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002638)  
I would like to address this question to our Secretary of the Budget.

**[02:58:07.320] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002639)  
Secretary De Leon, please.

**[02:58:10.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002640)  
Madam Chair, Your Honor,

**[02:58:13.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002641)  
following the pronouncement of our President, Ferdinand,

**[02:58:17.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002642)  
and our Marcos, Jr.,

**[02:58:18.720] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002643)  
to ensure the partial operability of the North-South Commuter Railway,

**[02:58:23.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002644)  
particularly the Northern Segment.

**[02:58:26.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002645)  
As a matter of fact, this project has the highest increase

**[02:58:31.460] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002646)  
if we compare it to the 2026 GAA level up to the 2027 NEP level.

**[02:58:39.060] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002647)  
So, for the information of our Honorable Pleyto for North-South Commuter Railway,

**[02:58:45.260] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002648)  
this includes the North-South Commuter Railway, the Northern Segment Railway, and the Northern Segment Railway.

**[02:58:47.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002649)  
This includes the North Portion and even the South Portion because these are systems.

**[02:58:51.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002650)  
The total proposal for the 2027 NEP is at P123.843 billion.

**[02:59:01.980] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002651)  
This includes P95 billion for its 2026 GAA level or about 330% increase.

**[02:59:10.740] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002652)  
This increase is far-reaching.

**[02:59:13.320] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002653)  
Our target is to achieve partial operability,

**[02:59:17.120] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002654)  
northern segment kaya po kailangan po mapabilis yun pong ongoing construction work para matupad

**[02:59:24.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002655)  
po yung target natin na partial operability by end of 2027.

**[02:59:47.120] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002656)  
Kailangan po kasi kasi kailangan talaga matapos sana ito at inaasahan po namin sa Bulacan at sa Pampanga.

**[02:59:55.580] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002657)  
Kailangan yung pondo nito huwag lahat na mabawasan Madam Chair at saka Secretary.

**[03:00:00.000] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002658)  
Honorable Chair, Your Honor, bagamat hindi po available sa amin sa ngayon yung breakdown between the North and the South segment,

**[03:00:11.600] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002659)  
in our discussions po, kung mababalikan po namin ng tama, malaking bahagi po ditong proposal is really for the Northern segment,

**[03:00:19.880] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002660)  
alinsunod po sa pronouncement po ng ating Pangulo.

**[03:00:22.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002661)  
But we'll get back to you on the exact figure, Your Honor, as to the breakdown of the 123.84 billion of the NECR system,

**[03:00:32.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002662)  
broken down based on the North segment and the South segment, Your Honor.

**[03:00:36.580] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002663)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Thank you, Secretary, for that response.

**[03:00:40.320] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002664)  
I will just take advantage, Madam Chair, nandito ang Secretary ng DepDev, Secretary Balisacan.

**[03:00:48.200] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002665)  
In connection to the very timely and urgent struggles of our fellow Filipinos

**[03:00:52.820] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002666)  
in the rising electricity rates, kaya gusto kong maitanong sa ating Secretary Balisakan,

**[03:01:02.500] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002667)  
kung mayroon ba tayong statistics o datos na nagpapakita kung gaano kalaking bahagi ng budget ng isang pamilya,

**[03:01:12.240] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002668)  
isang pamilyang Pilipino, ang napupunta sa kuryente, at lalo na sa mga mamamayan natin, mga pamilyang mababa ang kinikita.

**[03:01:21.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002669)  
At kapag tumataas ang presyo ng kuryente, ano ang nakikita nating pagbabago sa consumer behavior?

**[03:01:29.760] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002670)  
Binabawasan ba ng pamilya ang kanilang konsumo sa kuryente o alam naman natin, it's a basic necessity,

**[03:01:37.080] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002671)  
napipilitan lang silang magbawas ng gastos sa ibang pangangailangan gaya ng sa pagkain, edukasyon, healthcare, at iba pa.

**[03:01:45.080] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002672)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:01:46.140] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002673)  
Representative Pleyto

**[03:01:51.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002674)  
May I clarify to which agency that question is directed?

**[03:01:55.920] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002675)  
Secretary Balisacan, sa DepDev, Madam.

**[03:01:58.820] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002676)  
Yes, the household spending sa kuryente is quite sensitive sa price of electricity.

**[03:02:16.740] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002677)  
The share of electricity...

**[03:02:21.220] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002678)  
The share of electricity cost, expenditures of a typical household, an average household in the Philippines is 5.3%.

**[03:02:30.380] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002679)  
5.3%.

**[03:02:32.540] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002680)  
And that represents about something like 17,057 pesos per year.

**[03:02:41.740] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002681)  
Average, you know, it's much lower for the lower income groups.

**[03:02:49.920] DEPDev Secretary Arsenio M. Balisacan** (asr-002682)  
Madam Chair?

**[03:02:51.220] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002683)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[03:02:51.720] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002684)  
Salamat po sa inyong tugon, Sekretary.

**[03:02:56.740] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002685)  
Isa pang tanong na lang po, para mas maunawa na rin namin dito sa Kongreso,

**[03:03:02.040] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002686)  
nais ko po sanang makuha ang kasagutan ng aking susunod na tanong sa Central Bank of the Philippines.

**[03:03:08.820] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002687)  
Nandito po sila.

**[03:03:11.320] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002688)  
Since the prices of our goods and services are largely dependent on the prices of electricity,

**[03:03:18.260] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002689)  
kasi ang mga industrial at mga mamarati,

**[03:03:21.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002690)  
mga manufacturing sectors ay gumagamit din ng electricity and geoproduction.

**[03:03:27.500] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002691)  
Kumbaga, interrelated po ang mga sektor na ito sa paggalaw ng kuryente sa ating bansa.

**[03:03:35.320] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002692)  
Habang pataas ng pataas ang kuryente, pataas din ng pataas ang presyo ng bilihin.

**[03:03:41.680] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002693)  
Dahil kailangan may pasa ito ng mga sektor sa nabanggit kong mga consumers.

**[03:03:47.780] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002694)  
At ang pagdaloy o...

**[03:03:51.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002695)  
pass-through ay nagdudulot ng pagtaas na inflation.

**[03:03:55.180] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002696)  
How does the Banko Sentral ng Pilipinas resolve these issues of rising inflation?

**[03:04:00.000] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-002697)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:04:04.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002698)  
Deputy Governor, may please respond.

**[03:04:07.080] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002699)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:04:11.440] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002700)  
Talaga naman hong sinusubaybayan rin namin yung mga paggalaw ng iba't ibang components noong ating Consumer Price Index.

**[03:04:20.540] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002701)  
Kung saan.

**[03:04:21.200] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002702)  
Kung saan yun yung nagiging batayan sa pag-compute noong ating inflation rate.

**[03:04:26.780] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002703)  
Isa na ho dito yung sa electricity rates.

**[03:04:30.080] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002704)  
So tinitignan ho namin yun.

**[03:04:32.300] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002705)  
At tinitignan rin ho kung ito ba ay nagdudulot ng tinatawag na spillover sa iba pang mga presyo.

**[03:04:42.720] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002706)  
Paggalaw ng mga iba pang presyo na binibili ng ating mga kababayan.

**[03:04:49.320] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002707)  
Ito ho ay...

**[03:04:51.200] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002708)  
Closely, time team na tinitignan ho namin.

**[03:04:57.480] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002709)  
Gaya ho nang sinabi namin kanina, ang inflation rate ho at ganoon rin ho yung debt-debt.

**[03:05:03.340] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002710)  
Nakita ho na ito ay, ang average ho ay 5% this year.

**[03:05:08.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002711)  
Ngunit nung huling mga dalawang buwan, ito ay 6.4%, 6.2%.

**[03:05:15.280] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002712)  
Ang iba nito ho ay dulot rin rin noong sinasabi ninyong pag-akyat.

**[03:05:21.200] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002713)  
Noong ating inflation, o positive na inflation sa electricity rates.

**[03:05:27.440] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002714)  
So ito ho ay tinitignan namin ng mabuti.

**[03:05:30.260] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002715)  
At sa aming outlook po na binigay kanina,

**[03:05:33.420] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002716)  
ang pagsusuri ho namin, maaaring mag-average ng 6.4% this year ang ating inflation rate.

**[03:05:45.960] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002717)  
Kasama na ho dyan yung epekto noong pagtaas,

**[03:05:49.620] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002718)  
o yung mataas na...

**[03:05:51.200] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002719)  
presyo ng krudo sa international na merkado.

**[03:05:56.500] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002720)  
At ito ho, kasama na rin dyan yung epekto ho nito sa ating gasolina at saka sa electricity.

**[03:06:04.480] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002721)  
Kaya ho, medyo mataas yung nakikitang average na inflation ho itong taon na to.

**[03:06:12.380] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002722)  
Salamat po.

**[03:06:13.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002723)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Thank you sa Bangko Sentral ng Pilipinas for that response.

**[03:21:00.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002879)

**[03:21:02.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002880)  
So, ang punto po, sa halip na flood control, ilagay ang pondo sa classroom dahil 160,000 ang shortage ng classrooms, Madam Chair.

**[03:21:12.020] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002881)  
Thank you, Representative Tinio.

**[03:21:13.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002882)  
160,000.

**[03:21:14.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002883)  
That would be the last question for this round.

**[03:21:16.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002884)  
A response from...

**[03:21:19.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002885)  
Can you explain?

**[03:21:20.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002886)  
A response lang po, Madam Chair.

**[03:21:21.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002887)  
If Secretary De Leon would like to respond shortly, like a brief response to the query of Hon. Pleyto.

**[03:21:29.240] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002888)  
Madam Chair, Your Honor, dalawang mahalagang factor po patungkol po sa budget sa classroom.

**[03:21:33.940] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002889)  
Una po, yun pong 2026 budget of DepEd for classroom, yung kanilang absorptive capacity.

**[03:21:40.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002890)  
Ngayong second half pa lang po sila nag-uumpisang ma-implement yung kanilang nakalaang budget po para sa kanila na inaasahan pong magsispeel over.

**[03:21:48.500] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002891)  
Hanggang 2026.

**[03:21:49.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002892)  
Ang pangalawa pong mahalagang factor, Madam Chair, Your Honor, is by 2027, meron din po tayong isang package ng classroom infrastructure, school infrastructure project na tinatarget po nating ma-implement through a public-private partnership.

**[03:22:05.220] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002893)  
So ito po ay meron po tayong target na ah ito po yung PIP 3 na may estimated project cost na 105.7 billion na labas po on top po of our general appropriations act being a public

**[03:22:19.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002894)  
private partnership. Your Honor.

**[03:22:22.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002894-split-2)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Pag ah flood pag classroom hirap ma-implement pag flood control parang hindi. Yun lang po ang last comment ko, Madam Chair. Thank you.

**[03:22:33.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-002894-split-3)  
Again, sorry we've given you like 6 minutes 6 minutes 42 seconds over the time. So we move to the next interpellator from the majority representative Ryan Ro. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:22:49.380] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002895)  
Hello. Hello. Uh, good afternoon everyone. Oh, sorry.

**[03:22:58.070] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002895-split-2)  
Thank you po, Madam, Madam Chair. Thank you. I just have a question lang po to the DBM. Yes. Um, my question has to do with underspending and bottlenecks. Uh because the context of my question is our economy has slowed down by 2.3% second quarter of 2026. And the proposal currently increases

**[03:23:19.380] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002896)  
capital outlays by 13.8% but at the same time uh we have an issue with underspending and we based on our own calculations rin here yung uh ah there were resources that were shifted from GOCCs, nilagay po sa DPWH 27.33% to be exact. So yung tanong ko lang po is what mechanisms do we have in place

**[03:23:49.380] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002897)  
to to prevent underspending because we're increasing the budget but we haven't seemed to have solved the issue yet of underspending. So Secretary Kim, if you would enlighten us, please.

**[03:24:00.040] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002897-split-2)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair. Your Honor. Isa po sa napakahalagang pagbabago po na isinagawa po natin and when we talk about infrastructure projects, ito pong mga proyekto na nasa 2027 NEP gaya po ng nabanggit ko kanina already underwent a scrutiny in terms of completeness of documentary requirements.

**[03:24:19.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002898)  
So, ano po yung implication pag nasabi natin these projects have completed documentary requirements?

**[03:24:26.140] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002899)  
This means that the agencies responsible, not only DPWH but all the other agencies with infrastructure projects can actually undertake early procurement.

**[03:24:36.820] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002900)  
Because if we will observe, this is one thing that actually contributed to the delayed implementation of the infrastructure this year.

**[03:24:43.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002901)  
The late procurement for the infrastructure projects, that's why nakita po natin yung effects.

**[03:24:49.380] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002902)  
Sa economic growth for the last two quarters.

**[03:24:51.460] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002903)  
So, with the documentary requirements for 2027 projects complete and ongoing at this early, the agencies can actually start early procurement so that once the budget is passed, hoping that these proposals will be carried by Congress as early as January, they can proceed with awarding and immediate implementation of the respective infrastructure projects, Your Honor, Madam Chair.

**[03:25:16.300] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002904)  
So, follow-up lang po, Secretary Kim.

**[03:25:18.440] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002905)  
So, that mechanism...

**[03:25:19.380] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002906)  
So, that mechanism will extend, of course, na rin to next year, no?

**[03:25:21.900] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002907)  
That will be standard operating practice na po for the coming years, sir?

**[03:25:27.100] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002908)  
That is correct, Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[03:25:30.100] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002909)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:25:30.940] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002910)  
Thank you, Secretary Kim.

**[03:25:33.060] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002911)  
My next question lang po is to the DOF, Secretary Go.

**[03:25:37.200] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002912)  
Madam Chair, I notice in your figures, you projected an increase in revenue collections, quite optimistic, from 2026 to 2030.

**[03:25:47.900] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002913)  
However, given the...

**[03:25:49.380] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002914)  
In the context now of, nabanggat niyo po kanina, Mr. Secretary, yung progress bill that has around 85 billion in terms of revenue loss, kasi tatasin po natin yung threshold for personal income tax, tas magiging exempt po yung minimum corporate income tax.

**[03:26:09.020] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002915)  
But at the same time, excise taxes, you have the excise taxes will be increased, not just for sweetened beverages, but for vape products.

**[03:26:18.800] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002916)  
And among a lot of other things po, has those...

**[03:26:24.320] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002917)  
My understanding also is that currently there is...

**[03:26:27.980] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002918)  
Talks aren't even finished yet with regards to specific tax rates, like for vape, for example.

**[03:26:34.800] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002919)  
Nothing is final yet.

**[03:26:36.220] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002920)  
Have those considerations been included in the revenue collection calculations for next year at least, too, and even the year after that, Mr. Secretary?

**[03:26:47.680] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002921)  
Madam Chair, Your Honor.

**[03:26:48.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002922)  
The answer is we have not included the revenues from the proposed measures, revenue enhancing measures, and revenue eroding measures in progress.

**[03:27:01.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002923)  
They are not included in the projections that we have submitted.

**[03:27:08.080] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002924)  
Do we still expect collections to be...

**[03:27:13.580] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002925)  
To arrive at a good amount, given that, sir?

**[03:27:18.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002926)  
Your Honor, Madam Chair, a lot of it will depend on how the legislature finalizes the proposed numbers in the progress bill.

**[03:27:28.460] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002927)  
You are correct that there are essentially three measures that are revenue enhancing.

**[03:27:33.760] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002928)  
They affect social, environmental, and health taxes.

**[03:27:38.600] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002929)  
And, of course, we proposed a level of taxation, but we are very much aware that that will be subjected to intense...

**[03:27:46.800] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002930)  
Intense...

**[03:27:47.440] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002931)  
Scrutiny and intense discussions.

**[03:27:52.200] DOF Secretary Frederick D. Go** (asr-002932)  
So, I think we will not be able to answer that question until after all the deliberations on progress have been made.

**[03:28:02.140] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002933)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:28:03.880] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002934)  
And thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:28:05.600] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002935)  
My next question would be to the Banko Sentral ng Pilipinas.

**[03:28:11.280] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002936)  
You've posed inflation targets from 6.4 this year to 2027.

**[03:28:17.440] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002937)  
It will go down to 4.5 and then in 2028, it will be 3.1.

**[03:28:24.200] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002938)  
Isn't that a little too optimistic?

**[03:28:26.720] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002939)  
Because DepDev mentioned earlier, we have all of these other external circumstances hindering economic growth.

**[03:28:36.100] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002940)  
You have not just our domestic risks like underspending, but you still have the unresolved crisis in the Middle East,

**[03:28:42.780] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002941)  
global trade uncertainty, and volatile global capital flows.

**[03:28:47.440] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002942)  
What is your basis for setting targets for the downward trend in inflation from 4.5 to 3?

**[03:28:58.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002943)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:29:01.380] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002944)  
So, what we...

**[03:29:02.760] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002945)  
Ang ibinahagi ho namin kanina dun sa ating slides na 6.4 average inflation para sa taong ito, 2026,

**[03:29:13.240] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002946)  
at 4.5% sa 2027.

**[03:29:17.440] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002947)  
At 3.1% sa 2028, ito ho yung ating projections na ginawa noong huling Monetary Policy Meeting ng BSP.

**[03:29:30.240] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002948)  
Ito ho ay nangyari noong mga kalagitnaan ho ng Hunyo, 2026.

**[03:29:36.520] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002949)  
Ito ho ay isang forecast na ina-update namin every time na nag-meet ang Monetary Board ng BSP.

**[03:29:46.760] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002950)  
[unclear]

**[03:29:47.440] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002951)  
Ang target ho natin ay nananatiling 3% average inflation plus minus 1% na tolerance range.

**[03:30:00.000] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002952)  
Ngayon, hindi nagbabago yung target pero ang meron kami ay yung forecast natin.

**[03:30:10.560] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002953)  
Tama kayo na ang ating forecast, pababa ho ang trend ng ating inflation.

**[03:30:18.020] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002954)  
Kung ano ito, mataas yung inflation na nakikita natin dahil mas mataas siya sa 3% para sa taon na ito at sa 2027.

**[03:30:27.920] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002955)  
Ngayon, ano yung aming tinitignan na pwedeng maka-apekto dito sa ating inflation rate?

**[03:30:36.780] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002956)  
Pababa ho yung ating trend dahil ang nakalagay dyan, isa na rito,

**[03:30:44.660] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002957)  
pag titignan natin kung ano yung futures prices ng oil.

**[03:30:51.380] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002958)  
Ito ay mataas pero dahan-dahan bababa.

**[03:30:55.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002959)  
So, yun ay nakakatulong dito sa downward trend na ito.

**[03:31:03.020] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002960)  
Pangalawa, may mga ginagawa aksyon ng BSP na nakaka-apekto sa medium-term inflation.

**[03:31:13.200] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002961)  
Ang epekto ng mga huling policy actions ng BSP, inaabot ng 2-3 taon.

**[03:31:20.460] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002962)  
Ngayong taon na ito, nagtaas kami ng interest rates.

**[03:31:25.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002963)  
Ang total of 50 basis points.

**[03:31:29.620] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002964)  
Ang inaasahan ho sana namin dito ay ito ay makakapagpigil ng broader price pressures

**[03:31:39.080] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002965)  
isa-dalawang taon magmula ngayon.

**[03:31:43.380] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002966)  
Kahit ho na ito yung aming forecast,

**[03:31:49.820] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002967)  
pinahayag na rin ho ng Bangko Sentral na may mga risk factors na tinitignan.

**[03:31:55.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002968)  
Kung bakit maaaring mabagal yung pagbaba or gradual yung pagbaba ng inflation natin.

**[03:32:03.940] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002969)  
Isa na ho rito yung presyo ng crudo sa international market.

**[03:32:10.480] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002970)  
Pangalawa, yung pwede maging epekto ng El Nino na yung peak impact na ho ay mararanasan pa natin ng fourth quarter

**[03:32:20.480] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002971)  
until first half of 2027.

**[03:32:25.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002972)  
So, yun ho yung mga iba sa mga risk factors na nakikita ng Central Bank na pwede maka-epekto sa inflation natin.

**[03:32:36.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002973)  
Ito ho ay i-update namin dahil ito ho ay nangyari.

**[03:32:39.860] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002974)  
Yung forecast ho ay ginawa nung hunyo pa.

**[03:32:42.680] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002975)  
I-update ho ito next week ho pag nag-meet ulit ang Monetary Board ng Banko Sentral.

**[03:32:49.280] BSP Deputy Governor Zeno Ronald R. Abenoja** (asr-002976)  
Thank you.

**[03:32:50.000] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002977)  
Thank you, sir.

**[03:32:50.660] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002978)  
My concern lang there was I was just wondering if the targets were a little too optimistic.

**[03:32:55.680] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002979)  
Given all of those risk factors you've mentioned.

**[03:32:58.400] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002980)  
Pero good to hear naman po na ma-update naman po yan in the coming months while Congress works on the budget naman rin po also.

**[03:33:06.040] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002981)  
Last follow-up question na lang po.

**[03:33:09.180] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002982)  
Nabanggit nyo rin po kanina sa presentation po ninyo na yung Dubai crude oil, you're targeting 70 to 90 pesos.

**[03:33:18.620] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002983)  
Tama po ba?

**[03:33:19.880] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002984)  
And then, nung April, the Philippine Institute for Development Studies,

**[03:33:25.680] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002985)  
naglabas po sila ng paper.

**[03:33:29.320] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002986)  
At the time, the Dubai crude oil price was 105 pesos.

**[03:33:34.940] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002987)  
And if this year has taught us anything, a lot of things can happen talaga.

**[03:33:40.820] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002988)  
May mga contingency plans po ba?

**[03:33:42.900] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002989)  
Because the Middle East conflict is not yet over.

**[03:33:46.500] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002990)  
So moving forward, I guess my question is to the DBCC as a whole.

**[03:33:50.180] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002991)  
Are there contingency plans in place just in case tumaas?

**[03:33:55.680] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002992)  
At nagkakagulo pa ulit sa Middle East, tapos tumaas yung presyo ng crudo?

**[03:33:59.700] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002993)  
Kasi according to the PID study which I mentioned a while ago,

**[03:34:05.480] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002994)  
70 to 90 pesos, ah, 70 to 90 dollars, kapag tumaas lang yan to 105 dollars per crude oil,

**[03:34:12.600] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002995)  
that will be 1.4 million Filipinos are in danger of going below the poverty threshold na.

**[03:34:19.180] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002996)  
So would you please enlighten the committee?

**[03:34:21.360] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-002997)  
Thank you po.

**[03:34:22.680] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002998)  
Madam Chair, Your Honor, on the budgetary side,

**[03:34:25.640] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-002999)  
yun pong mga line items that we are actually using currently to respond to the oil crisis

**[03:34:32.840] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003000)  
are actually regular budget line items which are still present in the 2027 budget.

**[03:34:40.560] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003001)  
So in the event that there will be a similar event by 2027,

**[03:34:45.520] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003002)  
these line items stand ready to be mobilized to respond to the effects of the calamities,

**[03:34:50.640] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003003)  
including targeted subsidies to the most affected areas.

**[03:34:55.640] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003004)  
And vulnerable populations, some sort of fuel subsidy, including our farmers and fisher folks.

**[03:35:02.540] Acting DBM Secretary Kim Robert C. De Leon** (asr-003005)  
So ito naman po ay kasama po sa pinagandaan natin for the 2027 budget.

**[03:35:07.780] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-003006)  
Okay. Thank you so much. I believe my time is up. Pero yun na lang po. Maraming salamat.

**[03:35:12.600] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-003007)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:35:25.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003008)  
All right.

**[03:35:50.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003009)  
With that, we will have a break.

**[03:35:54.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003010)  
We'll end.

**[03:35:55.640] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003011)  
We'll end our morning session for now.

**[03:35:58.700] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003012)  
So again, it is 1 p.m.

**[03:36:00.820] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003013)  
We will suspend the briefing for now.

**[03:36:03.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003014)  
And may I request everyone to please take your lunch break and let us all be at Romualdez Hall by 2 o'clock in the afternoon.

**[03:36:12.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003015)  
And the first interpilator later from the minority would be Kong Eli San Fernando.

**[03:36:19.340] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003016)  
So again, thank you so much, everyone.

**[03:36:21.860] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003017)  
Please take your lunch.

**[03:36:23.380] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003018)  
And we'll resume at 2 p.m.

at Romualdez Hall.
